```
static
void kernel_atax(int m, int n,
		 DATA_TYPE POLYBENCH_2D(A,M,N,m,n),
		 DATA_TYPE POLYBENCH_1D(x,N,n),
		 DATA_TYPE POLYBENCH_1D(y,N,n),
		 DATA_TYPE POLYBENCH_1D(tmp,M,m))
{
  int i, j;

#pragma scop
  for (i = 0; i < _PB_N; i++)
    y[i] = 0;
  for (i = 0; i < _PB_M; i++)
    {
      tmp[i] = SCALAR_VAL(0.0);
      for (j = 0; j < _PB_N; j++)
	tmp[i] = tmp[i] + A[i][j] * x[j];
      for (j = 0; j < _PB_N; j++)
	y[j] = y[j] + A[i][j] * tmp[i];
    }
#pragma endscop
}
```

```
asm volatile(
    "movw %[A], %%si\n\t"
    "movw %[x], %%bx\n\t"
    "movw %[y], %%dx\n\t"

    "2:\n\t"
    "    fldz\n\t"
    "    movw %[N], %%cx\n\t"
    "    movw %[x], %%bx\n\t"
    "3:\n\t"
    "    fldl (%%si)\n\t"
    "    fmull (%%bx)\n\t"
    "    faddp\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%si\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%bx\n\t"
    "    decw %%cx\n\t"
    "    jnz 3b\n\t"
    "    fstpl (%%di)\n\t"
    "    subw %[rowstride], %%si\n\t"
    "    movw %[y], %%dx\n\t"
    "    movw %[N], %%cx\n\t"
    "4:\n\t"
    "    fldl (%%di)\n\t"
    "    fmull (%%si)\n\t"
    "    faddl (%%dx)\n\t"
    "    fstpl (%%dx)\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%si\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%dx\n\t"
    "    decw %%cx\n\t"
    "    jnz 4b\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%di\n\t"
    "    decw %%ax\n\t"
    "    jnz 2b\n"
    :
    : [A] "m" (A),
      [x] "m" (x),
      [y] "m" (y),
      [tmp] "m" (tmp),
      [N] "n" (_PB_N),
      [M] "n" (_PB_M),
      [dsize] "n" (sizeof(DATA_TYPE)),
      [rowstride] "n" (_PB_N * sizeof(DATA_TYPE))

);
```

/* Array initialization. */
static
void init_array (int m, int n,
		 DATA_TYPE POLYBENCH_2D(A,M,N,m,n),
		 DATA_TYPE POLYBENCH_1D(x,N,n))
{
  int i, j;
  DATA_TYPE fn;
  fn = (DATA_TYPE)n;

  for (i = 0; i < n; i++)
      x[i] = 1 + (i / fn);
  for (i = 0; i < m; i++)
    for (j = 0; j < n; j++)
      A[i][j] = (DATA_TYPE) ((i+j) % n) / (5*m);
}
```

Full final asm statement:

```
asm volatile(
    "movw %[A], %%si\n\t"
    "movw %[tmp], %%di\n\t"
    "movw %[y], %%bx\n\t"
    "movw %[N], %%cx\n\t"
    "testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "jz 9f\n\t"
    "1:\n\t"
    "    fldz\n\t"
    "    fstpl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%bx\n\t"
    "    decw %%cx\n\t"
    "    jnz 1b\n\t"
    "9:\n\t"
    "movw %[M], %%dx\n\t"
    "testw %%dx, %%dx\n\t"
    "jz 8f\n\t"
    "2:\n\t"
    "    fldz\n\t"
    "    movw %[N], %%cx\n\t"
    "    testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "    jz 7f\n\t"
    "    movw %[x], %%bx\n\t"
    "3:\n\t"
    "        fldl (%%si)\n\t"
    "        fmull (%%bx)\n\t"
    "        faddp\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%si\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%bx\n\t"
    "        decw %%cx\n\t"
    "        jnz 3b\n\t"
    "7:\n\t"
    "    fstpl (%%di)\n\t"
    "    subw %[rowstride], %%si\n\t"
    "    movw %[N], %%cx\n\t"
    "    testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "    jz 5f\n\t"
    "    movw %[y], %%bx\n\t"
    "4:\n\t"
    "        fldl (%%di)\n\t"
    "        fmull (%%si)\n\t"
    "        faddl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "        fstpl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%si\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%bx\n\t"
    "        decw %%cx\n\t"
    "        jnz 4b\n\t"
    "5:\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%di\n\t"
    "    decw %%dx\n\t"
    "    jnz 2b\n\t"
    "8:\n"
    :
    : [A] "m" (A),
      [x] "m" (x),
      [y] "m" (y),
      [tmp] "m" (tmp),
      [N] "n" (_PB_N),
      [M] "n" (_PB_M),
      [dsize] "n" (sizeof(DATA_TYPE)),
      [rowstride] "n" (_PB_N * sizeof(DATA_TYPE))
    : "memory", "cc", "bx", "cx", "dx", "si", "di"
);
```

For a double stored at memory address `M` little-endian: M+0 low word, M+2, M+4, M+6 high. To push a double so that in memory stack the double is little-endian with low word at lower address, you push high word first then lower words.     "3:\n\t"
    "    fldz\n\t"
    "    movw %[N], %%cx\n\t"
    "    testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "    jz 8f\n\t"

    "7:\n\t"
    "        fldl (%%si)\n\t"
    "        fmull (%%bx)\n\t"
    "        faddp\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%si\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%bx\n\t"

    "    testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "    jz 0f\n\t"
    "    movw %[y], %%bx\n\t"
    "9:\n\t"
    "        fldl (%%di)\n\t"
    "        fmull (%%si)\n\t"
    "        faddl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "        fstpl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%si\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%bx\n\t"
    "        decw %%cx\n\t"
    "        jnz 9b\n\t"
    "0:\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%di\n\t"
    "    decw %%dx\n\t"
    "5:\n\t"
    "    testw %%dx, %%dx\n\t"
    "    jz 6f\n\t"
    "    jmp 3b\n\t"
    "6:\n"
...
```

```
asm volatile(
    "movw %[A], %%si\n\t"
    "movw %[tmp], %%di\n\t"
    "movw %[y], %%bx\n\t"
    "movw %[N], %%cx\n\t"
    "testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "jz 2f\n\t"
    "1:\n\t"
    "    fldz\n\t"
    "    fstpl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%bx\n\t"
    "    decw %%cx\n\t"
    "    jnz 1b\n\t"
    "2:\n\t"
    "movw %[M], %%dx\n\t"
    "jmp 5f\n\t"
    "3:\n\t"
    "    fldz\n\t"
    "    fstpl (%%di)\n\t"
    "    movw %[N], %%cx\n\t"
    "    testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "    jz 8f\n\t"
    "    movw %[x], %%bx\n\t"
    "7:\n\t"
    "        fldl (%%si)\n\t"
    "        fmull (%%bx)\n\t"
    "        fldl (%%di)\n\t"
    "        faddp\n\t"
    "        fstpl (%%di)\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%si\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%bx\n\t"
    "        decw %%cx\n\t"
    "        jnz 7b\n\t"
    "8:\n\t"
    "    subw %[rowstride], %%si\n\t"
    "    movw %[N], %%cx\n\t"
    "    testw %%cx, %%cx\n\t"
    "    jz 0f\n\t"
    "    movw %[y], %%bx\n\t"
    "9:\n\t"
    "        fldl (%%di)\n\t"
    "        fmull (%%si)\n\t"
    "        faddl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "        fstpl (%%bx)\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%si\n\t"
    "        addw %[dsize], %%bx\n\t"
    "        decw %%cx\n\t"
    "        jnz 9b\n\t"
    "0:\n\t"
    "    addw %[dsize], %%di\n\t"
    "    decw %%dx\n\t"
    "5:\n\t"
    "    testw %%dx, %%dx\n\t"
    "    jz 6f\n\t"
    "    jmp 3b\n\t"
    "6:\n"
    :
    : [A] "m" (A),
      [x] "m" (x),
      [y] "m" (y),
      [tmp] "m" (tmp),
      [N] "n" (_PB_N),

      [rowstride] "n" (_PB_N * sizeof(DATA_TYPE))

);
```
